<a href="https://twitter.com/intent/follow?screen_name=deno_land" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/ca4b887411e152b9da8effb7d5ba1246a2f5562b08f090df9cc1de093a694a2d/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f747769747465722f666f6c6c6f772f64656e6f5f6c616e642e7376673f7374796c653d736f6369616c266c6162656c3d466f6c6c6f77" alt="Twitter badge" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/twitter/follow/deno_land.svg?style=social&amp;label=Follow" style="max-width: 100%;"></a> <a href="https://bsky.app/profile/deno.land" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/8200fd070bc6d20fd9ef6cbbb1956108e5b936ac65710f8031b48f916a3691c7/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f62616467652f466f6c6c6f772d7768697465736d6f6b653f6c6f676f3d626c7565736b79" alt="Bluesky badge" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/badge/Follow-whitesmoke?logo=bluesky" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
<a href="https://discord.gg/deno" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/7f137fd30f0d082ccf1db9eb79d83cea1d1381fac746180afe75a2fff7f95b72/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f646973636f72642f3638343839383636353134333230363038343f6c6f676f3d646973636f7264267374796c653d736f6369616c" alt="Discord badge" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/discord/684898665143206084?logo=discord&amp;style=social" style="max-width: 100%;"></a> <a href="https://www.youtube.com/@deno_land" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/364c81f143287abaf2f1a94d1965b049dfbd5c33c1cf9f4a57e6ddfeb4c1fb83/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f796f75747562652f6368616e6e656c2f73756273637269626572732f554371433247324d2d726734667a673165734b464c4649773f7374796c653d736f6369616c" alt="YouTube badge" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/youtube/channel/subscribers/UCqC2G2M-rg4fzg1esKFLFIw?style=social" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/1ae09995c55dbb006e9777d66c445b7a26c883b83b8869fe417f7ac4a3610b16/68747470733a2f2f64656e6f2e6c616e642f6c6f676f2e737667"><img align="right" src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/1ae09995c55dbb006e9777d66c445b7a26c883b83b8869fe417f7ac4a3610b16/68747470733a2f2f64656e6f2e6c616e642f6c6f676f2e737667" height="150px" alt="the deno mascot dinosaur standing in the rain" data-canonical-src="https://deno.land/logo.svg" style="max-width: 100%; height: auto; max-height: 150px;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://deno.com" rel="nofollow">Deno</a>
(<a href="https://ipa-reader.com/?text=%CB%88di%CB%90no%CA%8A" rel="nofollow">/ˈdiːnoʊ/</a>, pronounced
<code>dee-no</code>) is a JavaScript, TypeScript, and WebAssembly runtime with secure
defaults and a great developer experience. It's built on <a href="https://v8.dev/" rel="nofollow">V8</a>,
<a href="https://www.rust-lang.org/" rel="nofollow">Rust</a>, and <a href="https://tokio.rs/" rel="nofollow">Tokio</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Learn more about the Deno runtime
<a href="https://docs.deno.com/runtime/manual" rel="nofollow">in the documentation</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h2><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Install the Deno runtime on your system using one of the commands below. Note
that there are a number of ways to install Deno - a comprehensive list of
installation options can be found
<a href="https://docs.deno.com/runtime/manual/getting_started/installation" rel="nofollow">here</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Shell (Mac, Linux):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="curl -fsSL https://deno.land/install.sh | sh"><pre>curl -fsSL https://deno.land/install.sh <span class="pl-k">|</span> sh</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">PowerShell (Windows):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="irm https://deno.land/install.ps1 | iex"><pre>irm https:<span class="pl-k">//</span>deno.land<span class="pl-k">/</span>install.ps1 <span class="pl-k">|</span> iex</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://formulae.brew.sh/formula/deno" rel="nofollow">Homebrew</a> (Mac):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="brew install deno"><pre>brew install deno</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://community.chocolatey.org/packages/deno" rel="nofollow">Chocolatey</a> (Windows):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="choco install deno"><pre>choco install deno</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://winstall.app/apps/DenoLand.Deno" rel="nofollow">WinGet</a> (Windows):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="winget install --id=DenoLand.Deno"><pre>winget install <span class="pl-k">--</span>id<span class="pl-k">=</span>DenoLand.Deno</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://scoop.sh/#/apps?q=deno&amp;id=678d8fb557b611df996989c675b1099630a5bbee" rel="nofollow">Scoop</a>
(Windows):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-powershell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="scoop install main/deno"><pre>scoop install main<span class="pl-k">/</span>deno</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Build and install from source</h3><a id="user-content-build-and-install-from-source" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Build and install from source" href="#build-and-install-from-source"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Complete instructions for building Deno from source can be found
<a href="https://github.com/denoland/deno/blob/main/.github/CONTRIBUTING.md#building-from-source">here</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Your first Deno program</h2><a id="user-content-your-first-deno-program" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Your first Deno program" href="#your-first-deno-program"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Deno can be used for many different applications, but is most commonly used to
build web servers. Create a file called <code>server.ts</code> and include the following
TypeScript code:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-ts notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Deno.serve((_req: Request) =&gt; {
  return new Response(&quot;Hello, world!&quot;);
});"><pre><span class="pl-v">Deno</span><span class="pl-kos">.</span><span class="pl-en">serve</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s1">_req</span>: <span class="pl-smi">Request</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span> <span class="pl-c1">=&gt;</span> <span class="pl-kos">{</span>
  <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-k">new</span> <span class="pl-v">Response</span><span class="pl-kos">(</span><span class="pl-s">"Hello, world!"</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span>
<span class="pl-kos">}</span><span class="pl-kos">)</span><span class="pl-kos">;</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Run your server with the following command:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="deno run --allow-net server.ts"><pre>deno run --allow-net server.ts</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">This should start a local web server on
<a href="http://localhost:8000" rel="nofollow">http://localhost:8000</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Learn more about writing and running Deno programs
<a href="https://docs.deno.com/runtime/manual" rel="nofollow">in the docs</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Additional resources</h2><a id="user-content-additional-resources" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Additional resources" href="#additional-resources"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong><a href="https://docs.deno.com" rel="nofollow">Deno Docs</a></strong>: official guides and reference docs for
the Deno runtime, <a href="https://deno.com/deploy" rel="nofollow">Deno Deploy</a>, and beyond.</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://jsr.io/@std" rel="nofollow">Deno Standard Library</a></strong>: officially supported common
utilities for Deno programs.</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://jsr.io/" rel="nofollow">JSR</a></strong>: The open-source package registry for modern
JavaScript and TypeScript</li>
<li><strong><a href="https://deno.com/blog" rel="nofollow">Developer Blog</a></strong>: Product updates, tutorials, and
more from the Deno team.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Contributing</h2><a id="user-content-contributing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Contributing" href="#contributing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
